ABOUT CR POWER
關於華潤電力



China Resources Power Holdings Company Limited (the “Company”          華潤電力控股有限公司（「本公司」或「華
or “CR Power”), together with its subsidiaries (the “Group”) mainly    潤 電 力」）及 其 附 屬 公 司（「本 集 團」）主 要
invests, develops, operates and manages wind farms, photovoltaic       在中國投資、開發、運營和管理風電場、
power plants, hydro-electric power plants and other clean and          光伏電站、水電站及其他清潔及可再生能
renewable energy projects and coal-fired power plants in China. Its    源項目和燃煤發電廠。業務還涉及分佈式
business also covers distributed energy, power sales, intelligent      能源、售電、智慧能源及煤炭等領域。
energy, coal mining, and other areas.

As at 30 June 2024, CR Power had 48 coal-fired power plants, 185       於 2024 年 6 月 30 日，華 潤 電 力 旗 下 運 營
wind farms, 143 photovoltaic power plants, 28 hydro-electric power     48 座 燃 煤 發 電 廠、185 座 風 電 場、143 座
plants and 5 gas-fired power plants in commercial operations. Its      光伏電站、28 座水電站和 5 座燃氣發電廠。
total attributable operational generation capacity was 62,758 MW,      本公司的合計運營權益裝機容量為 62,758
with 27.0% of its capacity located in Central China, 25.9% in          兆 瓦，其 中 27.0% 位 於 華 中 地 區，25.9%
Eastern China, 16.3% in Southern China, 13.4% in Northern China,       位 於 華 東 地 區，16.3% 位 於 華 南 地 區，
7.5% in Northwestern China, 5.5% in Southwestern China, and            13.4% 位 於 華 北 地 區，7.5% 位 於 西 北 地
4.5% in Northeastern China. Its attributable operational generation    區，5.5% 位 於 西 南 地 區，以 及 4.5% 位 於
capacity in wind, hydro-electric and photovoltaic power combined,      東北地區。風電、水電及光伏發電運營權
amounted to 24,662 MW, accounting for approximately 39.3% of           益裝機容量合共 24,662 兆瓦，佔總運營權
its total attributable operational generation capacity.                益裝機容量約 39.3%。

CR Power has been selected, for five consecutive years, as a           自 2020 年 9 月 7 日起，華潤電力已連續五
constituent of the Hang Seng ESG 50 Index and the Hang Seng            年 入 選 恒 生 ESG 50 指 數 成 份 和 恒 生 可 持
Corporate Sustainability Benchmark Index (“HSSUSB”) since 7            續發展企業基準指數。
September 2020.

CR Power has always adhered to the concept of sustainable              華潤電力始終秉持可持續發展理念，將落
development and integrated the implementation of environmental,        實環境、社會和治理責任融入公司戰略和
social and governance responsibilities into the Company’s strategies   業 務 運 作，本 公 司 連 續 14 年 發 佈 可 持 續
and operations. The Company has been publishing sustainability         發展報告，不斷審視自身的表現和不足，
reports for 14 consecutive years, constantly reviews its own           努力提高踐行可持續發展的能力，為企業
performance and shortcomings, and strives to improve its sustainable   長遠的價值創造奠定基礎。連續五年入選
development practices, laying a solid foundation for creating long-    恒 生 ESG 50 指 數 以 及 恒 生 可 持 續 發 展 企
term value for the Company. CR Power’s inclusion into the Hang         業基準指數，彰顯了華潤電力在環境、社
Seng ESG 50 Index and HSSUSB for five consecutive years                會及企業管治三個範疇表現卓越，反映了
demonstrates its excellence in the three aspects of environment,       資本市場對本公司可持續發展工作的認可。
social and governance, and reflects the recognition on the
sustainable development efforts of the Company by the capital
markets.

CR Power has also been selected as a constituent in the Hang Seng      自 2023 年 6 月 5 日起，華潤電力亦被納入
Index since 5 June 2023. The Company’s efforts towards strategic       恒生指數成份股，充分顯示了資本市場對
transformation and innovative development are highly recognised by     於本公司轉型和創新發展的高度認可。
the capital markets.

million, of which approximately HK$12,638 million was used for the      用於風電場和光伏電站的建設，約 39.11
construction of wind farms and photovoltaic power plants,               億 港 元 用 於 火 電 機 組 的 建 設，約 5.35 億
approximately HK$3,911 million was used for the construction of         港元用於已運營發電機組的技術改造，約
thermal power units, approximately HK$535 million was used for the      3.52 億港元用於煤礦的建設，約 33.97 億
technological upgrades of operational power generation units,           港元用於綜合能源及其他項目的建設。

Carbon emission reduction                                              降低碳排放
The Group actively encourages the development of low-carbon            本 集 團 積 極 鼓 勵 碳 捕 集、利 用 與 封 存
technologies such as carbon capture, utilisation and storage (CCUS),   (CCUS) 等 低 碳 技 術 的 發 展，推 進 零 碳 園
promotes the construction of pilot zero-carbon parks, and optimises    區試點建設，優化碳資產管理模式。
the carbon assets management model.

The Group actively improves the integrated energy services and         積極改進綜合能源服務，聚焦企業、園區
focuses on enterprises, industrial parks and other energy-saving and   等節能減碳應用場景，結合自身豐富經驗
carbon reduction application scenarios, combined with its own          和先進技術，針對客戶用能需求，提供系
extensive experiences and advanced technologies, to provide            統化、定制化的綜合能源解決方案，助力
systematic and customised integrated energy solutions based on         企業、園區實現零碳排放，不斷提升綜合
customers’ energy needs, helping enterprises and industrial parks to   能源管理水平。
achieve zero carbon emission, and continuously enhancing integrated
energy management.

We firmly adhere to the concept of innovation-driven, focus on new     我們堅定秉承創新驅動理念，聚焦智慧儲
areas such as smart energy storage and virtual power plants, and       能、虛擬電廠等新領域，持續推進創新成
continue to promote the transformation of innovation achievements.     果轉化。

To facilitate the achievement of the carbon emission reduction         為促進碳減排目標的實施，本集團將碳減
targets, the Group explicitly includes carbon emission reduction and   排、可再生能源發展等明確列入高管團隊
renewable energy development in the performance contracts of the       業績合同，關鍵業績指標包括可再生能源
senior management, the key performance indicators include, among       權 益 裝 機 佔 比、新 增 可 再 生 能 源 併 網 容
others, the proportion of attributable generation capacity of          量、供電碳排放強度等，並根據實際完成
renewable energy, new grid-connected capacity of renewable energy      情況考核業績表現。
and the concentration of carbon emissions in power supply, and

Basis of Preparation of Financial Statements and                        財務報表的編製基準及主要會計政策
Principal Accounting Policies
These condensed consolidated financial statements have been             本簡明合併財務報表已根據香港會計師
prepared in accordance with Hong Kong Accounting Standard 34            公會頒佈的香港會計準則第 34 號（「香港
(“HKAS 34”) “Interim Financial Reporting” issued by Hong Kong           會計準則第 34 號」）「中期財務報告」及香
Institute of Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”) as well as with    港聯合交易所有限公司證券上市規則（「上
the applicable disclosure requirements of Appendix D2 to the Rules      市規則」）附錄 D2 的適用披露規定而編製。
Governing the Listing of Securities on The Stock Exchange of Hong
Kong Limited (the “Listing Rules”).

As at 30 June 2024, the Group had net current liabilities of            本 集 團 於 2024 年 6 月 30 日 擁 有 流 動 負 債
HK$23,749 million. The directors of the Board (“Directors”) are of      淨 額 237.49 億 港 元。董 事 認 為，經 考 慮
the opinion that, taking into account the current operation of the      本集團當前的經營以及本集團可供動用
Group as well as the banking facilities available to the Group, the     的銀行授信，本集團有足夠營運資金悉數
Group has sufficient working capital to enable it to meet in full its   履行其由簡明合併財務狀況表日期起計
financial obligations as and when they fall due in the coming twelve    未來十二個月到期的財務責任。因此，該
months from the date of the condensed consolidated statement of         等簡明合併中期財務資料已按持續經營
financial position. Therefore, such condensed consolidated interim      基準編製。

Foreign exchange risk                                                                        匯率風險
The Group collects substantially all of its revenue in RMB and most of                       本集團的收入絕大部分以人民幣收取，本
its expenditures, including expenditures incurred in the operation of                        集團的大部分支出（包括於經營發電廠時
power plants as well as capital expenditures, are denominated in                             產生的支出及資本支出）亦以人民幣計算。
RMB. Dividends receivable from the Company’s subsidiaries and                                而來自本公司附屬公司及聯營企業的應
associates may be collected in either RMB, HKD or GBP.                                       收股息則可以人民幣、港元或英鎊收取。

RMB is not a freely convertible currency. Future exchange rates of                           人民幣並非自由兌換貨幣。人民幣的未來
the RMB may vary significantly from the current or historical                                匯率可能與現行或過往的匯率有重大差
exchange rates. The exchange rates may also be affected by                                   異。匯率亦可能受經濟發展及政治變動以
economic developments and political changes and supply and                                   及人民幣供求關係影響。人民幣兌港元及
demand of the RMB. The appreciation or depreciation of the RMB                               美元升值或貶值可能對本集團的經營業
against the HKD or the USD may have positive or negative impact on                           績造成正面或負面影響。
the results of operations of the Group.

The functional currency of major project companies of the Group is                           本集團主要項目公司的功能性貨幣為人
RMB, and their revenue and expenses are mainly denominated in                                民幣，其收益及支出主要以人民幣列值，

denominated in HKD. However, certain entities are located in Hong                            實體位於香港，其功能貨幣為港元，其匯
Kong and their functional currencies are HKD. Their foreign exchange                         率風險主要源於以人民幣列值的結餘和
risk mainly arises from balances denominated in RMB and borrowings                           人民幣借款。
denominated in RMB.

consolidated financial statements in accordance with HKAS 34. Our         條款向閣下（作為整體）報告我們的結論，
responsibility is to express a conclusion on these condensed              除此之外本報告別無其他目的。我們不會
consolidated financial statements based on our review, and to report      就本報告的內容向任何其他人士負上或
our conclusion solely to you, as a body, in accordance with our           承擔任何責任。
agreed terms of engagement, and for no other purpose. We do not
assume responsibility towards or accept liability to any other person
for the contents of this report.

SCOPE OF REVIEW                                                           審閱範圍
We conducted our review in accordance with Hong Kong Standard             我們已根據香港會計師公會頒佈的香港
on Review Engagements 2410 “Review of Interim Financial                   審閱準則第 2410 號「由實體的獨立核數師

issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants. A      該等簡明合併財務報表包括主要向負責
review of these condensed consolidated financial statements consists      財務和會計事務的人士作出查詢，及應用
of making inquiries, primarily of persons responsible for financial and   分析性和其他審閱程序。審閱的範圍遠較
accounting matters, and applying analytical and other review              根據《香港 審計準則》進行審計的範圍為
procedures. A review is substantially less in scope than an audit         小，故不能令我們可保證我們將知悉在審

31. FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENTS OF                                                      31. 金融工具的公允價值計量
    FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS
      Fair value measurements                                                          公允價值計量
      The fair values of these financial assets and financial liabilities              該等金融資產及金融負債的公允價
      are determined (in particular, the valuation technique(s) and                    值已予釐定（尤其是所使用的估值技
      inputs used), as well as the level of the fair value hierarchy into              術及輸入數據），以及公允價值計量
      which the fair value measurements are categorised (Levels 1 to                   的公允價值層級（第一至三級）乃根
      3) based on the degree to which the inputs to the fair value                     據公允價值計量的輸入數據的可觀
      measurements is observable.                                                      察程度而釐定。

      •        Level 1 fair value measurements are based on quoted                     •     第一級公允價值計量乃基於相
               prices (unadjusted) in active market for identical assets or                  同資產或負債在活躍市場的報
               liabilities;                                                                  價（未經調整）；

      •        Level 2 fair value measurements are those derived from                  •     第二級公允價值計量來自資產
               inputs other than quoted prices included within Level 1                       或負債的直接（即價格）或間接
               that are observable for the asset or liability, either directly              （即源自價格）可觀察輸入數據
               (i.e. as prices) or indirectly (i.e. derived from prices);                   （第一級包括的報價除外）；

      •        Level 3 fair value measurements are those derived from                  •     第三級公允價值計量指來自估
               valuation techniques that include inputs for the asset or                     值 技 術 的 計 量，包 括 並 非 基 於
               liability that are not based on observable market data                        可觀察市場數據釐定的資產或
               (unobservable inputs).                                                        負債的輸入數據（不可觀察輸入
                                                                                             數據）。

      Fair value of the Group’s financial assets and                                   本集團按經常性基準以公允價值
      financial liabilities that are measured at fair value                            計量的金融資產及金融負債的公
      on a recurring basis                                                             允價值

                                           Fair value as at
                                        於以下日期的公允價值

   financial assets                                                  對賬

                                                                                                 assets at

2023, the position of the President has become vacant. Mr. Shi, the       空。主席史先生暫時承擔過渡期間總裁的
Chairman, assumes the interim duties and responsibilities of the          工作及責任，故本公司未能符合守則條文

Provision C.2.1. The Company is endeavouring to identify suitable         可行情況下盡快物色具備適當專業資格
candidate(s) with appropriate professional qualifications or relevant     或 相 關 專 長 之 合 適 人 選，以 獲 委 任 為 總
expertise to be appointed as the President as soon as practicable. The    裁。本公司將於適當時候就上述事宜作出
Company will make further announcement(s) in relation to the above        進一步公告。
matters as and when appropriate.
